SELECT o."Id", o."VisitId", o."Priority", b0."Name" AS "BrandName", c0."CustomerName" AS "DistributorName", o1."VehicleNumber", o."RequestedQuantity", o."RemainingQuantity", o."CompletedQuantity", o."CreatedAt"
FROM aquora_tenant_code_company."OperationsFillingQueues" AS o
INNER JOIN (
    SELECT b."Id", b."Name"
    FROM aquora_tenant_code_company."Brands" AS b
    WHERE NOT (b."IsDeleted")
) AS b0 ON o."BrandId" = b0."Id"
INNER JOIN (
    SELECT c."Id", c."CustomerName"
    FROM aquora_tenant_code_company."Customers" AS c
    WHERE NOT (c."IsDeleted")
) AS c0 ON o."DistributorId" = c0."Id"
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT o0."Id", o0."VehicleNumber"
    FROM aquora_tenant_code_company."OperationsVisits" AS o0
    WHERE NOT (o0."IsDeleted")
) AS o1 ON o."VisitId" = o1."Id"
WHERE NOT (o."IsDeleted") AND o."TenantId" = @tenantId AND o."Status" <> 'Completed' AND o."Status" <> 'Cancelled'
ORDER BY CASE
    WHEN o."Priority" = 'Immediate' THEN 3
    WHEN o."Priority" = 'High' THEN 2
    ELSE 1
END DESC, o."CreatedAt"

2026-07-21 19:26:14.109 +05:30 [INF] Executed DbCommand (150ms) [Parameters=[@tenantId='244655ac-96d5-4251-a482-90c932356adb'], CommandType='"Text"', CommandTimeout='120']
SELECT o."Id", o."VisitId", o."Priority", b0."Name" AS "BrandName", c0."CustomerName" AS "DistributorName", o1."VehicleNumber", o."RequestedQuantity", o."RemainingQuantity", o."CompletedQuantity", o."CreatedAt"
FROM aquora_tenant_code_company."OperationsFillingQueues" AS o
INNER JOIN (
    SELECT b."Id", b."Name"
    FROM aquora_tenant_code_company."Brands" AS b
    WHERE NOT (b."IsDeleted")
) AS b0 ON o."BrandId" = b0."Id"
INNER JOIN (
    SELECT c."Id", c."CustomerName"
    FROM aquora_tenant_code_company."Customers" AS c
    WHERE NOT (c."IsDeleted")
) AS c0 ON o."DistributorId" = c0."Id"
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT o0."Id", o0."VehicleNumber"
    FROM aquora_tenant_code_company."OperationsVisits" AS o0
    WHERE NOT (o0."IsDeleted")
) AS o1 ON o."VisitId" = o1."Id"
WHERE NOT (o."IsDeleted") AND o."TenantId" = @tenantId AND o."Status" <> 'Completed' AND o."Status" <> 'Cancelled'
ORDER BY CASE
    WHEN o."Priority" = 'Immediate' THEN 3
    WHEN o."Priority" = 'High' THEN 2
    ELSE 1
END DESC, o."CreatedAt"
